# Publication coverage matrix

**Repository:** `leoinfer/remora-lab`<br>
**Visibility:** public GitHub repository<br>
**Generated:** `2026-08-27T12:30:52Z`<br>
**Records:** `1092`<br>
**UNACCOUNTED:** `0`

**Current Flash-Next campaign:** represented by `CAMPAIGN-*` records and
[`research/flash-next/CURRENT_CAMPAIGN.md`](research/flash-next/CURRENT_CAMPAIGN.md).

This is the authoritative final closure crosswalk for the declared whole-machine archaeology boundary. It records normalized idea-index entries, source-register entries, artifact-register entries, source trees, root archaeology files, HAR worktree records, the read-only HDD aggregate, current Flash-Next assets, and an explicit mandatory-family crosswalk. A record can be public, pending, excluded, superseded, or falsified-but-public; `UNACCOUNTED = 0` means every discovered unit in the declared boundary has one of those decisions.

No private path, hostname, branch name, machine identifier, model payload, raw receipt, or opaque archive content is copied into this matrix. Source register IDs intentionally replace private locations.

## Required field definitions

Every row below contains the exact required fields: `ID`, `NAME`, `CATEGORY`, `SOURCE LOCATION / SOURCE REGISTER ID`, `CURRENT STATUS`, `PUBLIC DISPOSITION`, `PUBLIC PATH`, `IMPLEMENTATION PATH`, `PROVENANCE STATUS`, `PRIVACY STATUS`, `LICENSE STATUS`, `CLAIM STATUS`, `REASON IF OMITTED`, and `FOLLOW-UP REQUIRED`.

Provenance is deliberately conservative. `NOVELTY_NOT_ESTABLISHED` is used when the archaeology does not prove originality; `INSPIRED`, `ADAPTED`, `DERIVED`, `INDEPENDENT_REDISCOVERY`, `ORIGIN_UNCERTAIN`, and `ORIGINAL_TO_PROJECT` are not interchangeable. The matrix never infers novelty from local authorship alone.
